\displaystyle\begin{split}&O^{(2)\,\text{shift}}_{2,\text{ single-UV}}(p,q,k,l)=-ig_{s}^{3}\,\frac{C_{A}}{2}\,\frac{1}{l^{2}(l-q)^{2}(p+l)^{2}}\left[\not{l}(\not{p}+\not{l})\,\Pi^{(1)\,\text{shift}}_{\,\text{UV}}(l,k)\right.\\
&\qquad+\left.\Pi^{(1)\,\text{shift}}_{\,\text{UV}}(l-q,k)(\not{p}+\not{l})(\not{q}-\not{l})\right]\,,\end{split}

(345)

and

\displaystyle O^{(2)\,\text{shift}}_{\Delta,2\text{ single-UV}}(p,q,k,l)=ig_{s}^{5}\,C_{A}^{2}\,\frac{(1-\epsilon)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{\not{p}+\not{k}+\not{q}}{k^{2}(p+k+q)^{2}}-\frac{\not{p}+\not{k}}{(q-k)^{2}(p+k)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-ig_{s}^{3}\,\frac{C_{A}}{2}\,\frac{\Pi^{(1)\,\text{shift}}_{\,\text{UV}}(q,k)}{l^{2}(l-q)^{2}}\,.

(346)

F.2
Double-
uv
counterterms at two loops.

After treating the single-UV region in which the two-loop amplitude diverges when one of the loop momenta approaches infinity, we turn our attention to the double-UV limit, in which both loop momenta
k
,
l
become infinitely large.
Below, we will find it particularly convenient to symmetrise the kinematic functions
13
13

13

Antisymmetric terms in the loop momenta
k,l
vanish and the numerator
\mathcal{N}^{\mu}_{qqg\text{ double-UV}}
belonging to the double-UV approximation of the three-point function,
\Gamma^{(2)\,\mu}_{qqg\text{ double-UV}}
, satisfies
\mathcal{N}^{\mu}_{qqg\text{ double-UV}}=f_{1}^{\mu}\not{k}+f_{2}^{\mu}\not{l}+f_{3}^{\mu}\gamma^{\mu}
, where
f_{i}^{\mu}=f_{i}^{\mu}(k,l)
.
in the loop-momenta
k
and
l
.

We begin with the uncrossed ladder vertex functions
V_{i}^{(2,UL)\,\mu}
, defined in eqs. (
264
)-(
266
). Though strictly speaking a symmetrisation is not required since the integrand is simple enough, we find it convenient in the application of Ward identities in the collinear regions. Since
V_{1}^{(2,UL)\,\mu}
and
V_{2}^{(2,UL)\,\mu}
have the same colour coefficient both as part of the electroweak and QCD vertex, we find it useful to combine them,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,\left(V_{1\text{ double-UV}}^{(2,UL)\,\mu}(k,l)+V_{2\text{ double-UV}}^{(2,UL)\,\mu}(k,l)\right)\\
&\qquad=\frac{2(1-\epsilon)^{2}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left[\frac{4l^{\mu}\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right.\right.\\
&\qquad\left.\left.-\frac{2k\cdot l}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(1-\frac{2k\cdot l}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right)+\frac{2k^{\mu}\not{l}+2l^{\mu}\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.\times\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\left(1-\frac{2k\cdot l}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)-\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\gamma^{\mu}\left(\frac{1}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}-\frac{2k\cdot l}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(1-\frac{2k\cdot l}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\right.\\
&\qquad\left.\left.+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}-\frac{1}{(l^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right)\right]\\
&\qquad+2(1-\epsilon)\frac{V^{(1)\,\mu}_{\text{UV}}(l)}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,.\end{split}

(347)

Here, we have introduced a symmetrisation operator
\mathscr{S}_{k,l}
as follows,

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}_{l,k}\,\mathcal{I}(l,k)=\frac{1}{2}\left(\mathcal{I}(l,k)+(l\leftrightarrow k)\right)\,.

(348)

where
\mathcal{I}
is a kinematic function of the loop momenta
k
and
l
.
The remaining double-UV approximation reads

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,V_{3\text{ double-UV}}^{(2,UL)\,\mu}(k,l)\\
&\qquad=\frac{1-\epsilon}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\left[V^{(1)\,\mu}_{\text{UV}}(l)\left(\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)+\frac{2k^{\mu}k_{\alpha}V^{(1)\,\alpha}_{\text{UV}}(l)}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{4(1-\epsilon)(l+k)^{\mu}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\left(\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{\not{k}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\right.\\
\ &\qquad\left.\left.-\frac{\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\right)\right]+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,.\end{split}

(349)

Then, the double-UV counterterm for the two-loop uncrossed ladder contribution to the two-point function can be written as,

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,S^{(2,UL)}_{\text{double-UV}}(p,k,l)=-p_{\mu}\left(\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,V^{(2,UL)\,\mu}_{\text{double-UV}}(k,l)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\{\frac{2(1-\epsilon)^{2}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left[\left(\frac{\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{\not{k}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{2l\cdot k}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\right.\right.\right.

(350)

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\left.\left.-\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)-\frac{\not{l}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}+\frac{4(l\cdot k)^{2}\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\right]+(k\leftrightarrow l)\right\}\,.

For the crossed ladder contributions we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,\left(V_{1\text{ double-UV}}^{(2,XL)\,\mu}(k,l)+V_{2\text{ double-UV}}^{(2,XL)\,\mu}(k,l)\right)\\
&\qquad=\frac{2(1-\epsilon)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left[\frac{4(l+k)^{\mu}(\not{l}+\not{k})}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.+\frac{4\epsilon\,l^{\mu}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\left(\frac{\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{\not{k}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\\
&\qquad\left.+\frac{\epsilon\gamma^{\mu}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}+\frac{4l^{\mu}(\not{l}+\not{k})}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{4(1+\epsilon)l^{\mu}\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(k^{2}-M^{2})}+\frac{8k\cdot l\,l^{\mu}\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\right]+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,\end{split}

(351)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,V_{3\text{ double-UV}}^{(2,XL)\,\mu}(k,l)\\
&\qquad=-\frac{2(1-\epsilon)}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})}\left[\frac{2\epsilon\,l^{\mu}\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.+\frac{2(l+k)^{\mu}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\left(\frac{\not{l}+\not{k}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{2\not{l}+2(1+\epsilon)\not{k}}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{(2+\epsilon)\gamma^{\mu}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{2(l^{2}-M^{2})}-\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right]+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,.\end{split}

(352)

Then, the corresponding crossed self-energy contribution can be written as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,S^{(2,XL)}(p,k,l)=-p_{\mu}\left(\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,V^{(2,XL)\,\mu}_{\text{double-UV}}(k,l)\right)\\
&\qquad-\frac{2(1-\epsilon)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left[\frac{\not{l}+\not{k}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1+\epsilon}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{2}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{2\epsilon\,\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}-\frac{4l\cdot k\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(k^{2}-M^{2})}+(k\leftrightarrow l)\right]\,.\end{split}

(353)

Next, we turn our attention to the three-gluon vertex contribution, whose three-point function in the double-UV limit reads,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,V^{(2,3V)\,\mu}(k,l)=\frac{2(1-\epsilon)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left[\frac{4l^{\mu}(\not{l}+\not{k})}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(k^{2}-M^{2})}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{(\not{l}-\not{k})(l-k)^{\mu}-2l^{\mu}\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}+\frac{4l^{\mu}(\not{l}-\not{k})}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{\gamma^{\mu}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{2}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\right)+\frac{2V^{(1)\,\mu}_{\text{UV}}(k)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{2l^{\mu}l_{\alpha}V^{(1)\,\alpha}_{\text{UV}}(k)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\right]+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,.\end{split}

(354)

The corresponding self-energy contribution can be written as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,S^{(2,3V)}(p,k,l)=-p_{\mu}\left(\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,V^{(2,3V)\,\mu}_{\text{double-UV}}(k,l)\right)\\
&\qquad+\frac{2(1-\epsilon)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left[\frac{\not{l}-\not{k}}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{\not{l}+\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}+\frac{4l\cdot k\not{l}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}+(k\leftrightarrow l)\right]\,.\end{split}

(355)

If the external particle is a gluon, we have additional non-abelian topologies which contribute to the double-UV limits. We begin with the four-gluon vertex function, defined in eq. (
278
). It has a particularly simple form if we symmetrise in
k
and
l
,

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,W^{(2,4V)\,\mu}_{\text{double-UV}}(k,l)=-\frac{2(1-\epsilon)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left[\frac{2l^{\mu}\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\frac{\gamma^{\mu}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{2}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right]+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,.

(356)

Next, we discuss the uncrossed-ladder-type topology with a three-gluon vertex, denoted by
\Gamma^{(2,UL-3V)}
, consisting of two diagrams shown on the right-hand side of eq. (
279
).
The kinematic function
W_{1}^{(2,UL)}
belonging to the first diagram in eq. (
279
) has the following expansion in the double-UV limit,

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,Q^{(2,UL)\,\mu}_{1,\text{ double-UV}}(k,l)=\frac{4(1-\epsilon)^{2}l^{\mu}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\left[\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{\not{k}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.-\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\not{k}-\frac{2l\cdot k\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{2l\cdot k}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)+\frac{\not{l}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,.

(357)

Likewise, the contribution associated to ghosts has the double-UV counterterm,

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,O^{(2,UL)\,\mu}_{1,\text{ double-UV}}(k,l)=\frac{1-\epsilon}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\Biggl\{\frac{2l^{\mu}\not{k}-2k^{\mu}\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\Biggl[\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\Biggl(1-\frac{2l\cdot k}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\Biggr]+\gamma^{\mu}\Biggl[\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\Biggl(\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\Biggr)+\frac{2}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}+\frac{2l\cdot k}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\Biggl(1-\frac{2l\cdot k}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\Biggr)\Biggr]\Biggr\}

(358)

\displaystyle\qquad+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,.

The term
W_{2}^{(2,UL)}
belonging to the second diagram in eq. (
279
), has the following double-UV scalar and ghost contributions,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,Q^{(2,UL)\,\mu}_{2,\text{ double-UV}}(k,l)=\frac{4(1-\epsilon)^{2}k^{\mu}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left[\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{\not{k}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right]+(1-\epsilon)\left(\frac{Q_{\text{UV}}^{(1)\,\mu}(k)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}-\frac{2l^{\mu}l_{\alpha}Q_{\text{UV}}^{(1)\,\alpha}(k)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\right)+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,\end{split}

(359)

and

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,O^{(2,UL)\,\mu}_{2,\text{ double-UV}}(k,l)=\frac{(1-\epsilon)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left[\frac{4(k+l)^{\mu}\not{l}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.-\frac{4l^{\mu}\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}+\frac{\gamma^{\mu}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right]+(1-\epsilon)\left(\frac{O_{\text{UV}}^{(1)\,\mu}(k)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}-\frac{2l^{\mu}l_{\alpha}O_{\text{UV}}^{(1)\,\alpha}(k)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,

(360)

where
Q^{(1)}_{\text{UV}}
and
O^{(1)}_{\text{UV}}
are defined in eqs. (
53
) and (
54
).

Similarly, the scalar and ghost parts of the uncrossed-ladder-type contributions read,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,Q^{(2,XL)\,\mu}_{\text{ double-UV}}(k,l)=\frac{2(1-\epsilon)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left\{\frac{2(l+k)^{\mu}(\not{l}+\not{k})}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.+\frac{8l\cdot kl^{\mu}\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}-\frac{4\epsilon l^{\mu}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\left[\frac{\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\right.\right.\\
&\qquad\left.\left.-\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{\not{k}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right]\right.\\
&\qquad\left.-\frac{4l^{\mu}(\not{l}+\not{k})}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right\}+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,\end{split}

(361)

and

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,O^{(2,XL)\,\mu}_{\text{ double-UV}}(k,l)=\frac{2(1-\epsilon)}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left\{\frac{(l+k)^{\mu}(\not{l}+\not{k})}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((l+k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\frac{l^{\mu}\not{k}-k^{\mu}\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.-\frac{2l^{\mu}\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\gamma^{\mu}\left[\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\left.+\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{2l\cdot k}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left(1-\frac{2l\cdot k}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)+\frac{3}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\left.\left.-\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right]\right\}+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,

(362)

where we have defined,
X^{\mu}=X_{1}^{\mu}+X_{2}^{\mu}
for
X\in\{Q^{(2,XL)},O^{(2,XL)}\}
. In a similar fashion, the symmetrised double-UV counterterms for the double three-gluon vertex part is

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,Q^{(2,d3V)}_{\text{double-UV}}(k,l)=\frac{8(1-\epsilon)k^{\mu}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left[\frac{\not{l}-\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\right.\\
&\qquad\left.+\frac{\not{l}-\not{k}}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)+\frac{2l\cdot k\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(k^{2}-M^{2})}\right]\\
&\qquad+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,\end{split}

(363)

and

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,O^{(2,d3V)}_{\text{double-UV}}(k,l)=\frac{\gamma^{\mu}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{2}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{3}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.-4(1-\epsilon)\frac{(l\cdot k)^{2}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(k^{2}-M^{2})}\right)-\frac{1-\epsilon}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left\{\frac{4l^{\mu}\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\frac{2(k^{\mu}\not{l}-l^{\mu}\not{k})}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left(\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\frac{2(k-2l)^{\mu}\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}-\gamma^{\mu}\left[\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\left.-\frac{1}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\left(1+\frac{2l\cdot k}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right]\right\}+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,

(364)

where
X^{\mu}=X_{1}^{\mu}+X_{2}^{\mu}
for
X\in\{Q^{(2,d3V)},O^{(2,d3V)}\}
.

Finally, we provide the (symmetrised) double-UV approximations for the shift terms, including ghost contributions. The uncrossed ladder contribution, given in eq. (
75
), yields the counterterm,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,\Pi_{qq\text{ double-UV}}^{(2,UL)\,\text{shift}}(q,k,l)=ig_{s}^{4}\,(1-\epsilon)^{2}\left[C_{A}C_{F}\,s^{(UL)}_{1}(q,k,l)\right.\\
&\qquad\left.+C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)s^{(UL)}_{2}(q,k,l)\right]+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,\end{split}

(365)

with coefficients
s^{(UL)}_{1}
and
s^{(UL)}_{2}
, corresponding to loop momentum shifts of the “outer” and “inner” loops, given by

\displaystyle s^{(UL)}_{1}(q,k,l)=\frac{6k\cdot q\,\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{4}}\left[\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(k+l)^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{2k\cdot l}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(1-\frac{2k\cdot l}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\left[\frac{\not{q}}{(k+l)^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{2k\cdot q\,(\not{k}-2\not{l})}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(k+l)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-\frac{2(k+l)\cdot q\,\not{k}}{((k+l)^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}-\frac{4k\cdot l\,(k\cdot l\,\not{q}+4k\cdot q\,\not{l})}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\right]-\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left[\frac{2(k+l)\cdot q\,\not{k}}{((k+l)^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\not{q}+\frac{2l\cdot q\,\not{l}}{(k+l)-M^{2}}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\not{q}+2\frac{k\cdot l\,\not{q}+k\cdot q\,\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\,,

(366)

and

\displaystyle s^{(UL)}_{2}(q,k,l)=-\frac{2k\cdot q}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{4k\cdot l\,\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-3\not{k}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((k+l)^{2}-M^{2})}\left(\not{q}-\frac{2{(k+l)\cdot q\,}\not{k}}{(k+l)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left[2k\cdot q\left(\frac{\not{l}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{\not{k}}{(k+l)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)-\not{q}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2{(k+l)\cdot q\,}\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}((k+l)^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\,.

(367)

The double-UV counterterm for the crossed-ladder shift term, defined in eq. (
78
), is given by,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,\Pi_{qq\text{ double-UV}}^{(2,XL)\,\text{shift}}(q,k,l)=ig_{s}^{4}\,2C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)(1-\epsilon)\,s^{(XL)}(q,k,l)\\
&\qquad+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,\end{split}

(368)

with

\displaystyle s^{(XL)}(q,k,l)=\frac{2{k\cdot q}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}(l^{2}-M^{2})}\left(2\,\frac{(1+\epsilon)\not{k}+\not{l}}{(k+l)^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{2(\not{k}+\not{l})+(1+2\epsilon)\not{k}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{4k\cdot l\,\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left((1+\epsilon)\not{q}+2\,\frac{k\cdot q\,(\not{k}+(3+\epsilon)\not{l})-2l\cdot q\,\not{k}}{(k+l)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((k+l)^{2}-M^{2})}\left(4(k+l)\cdot q\,\frac{(1+\epsilon)\not{l}+\not{k}}{(k+l)^{2}-M^{2}}-(2+\epsilon)\not{q}\right)

(369)

Similarly, for the
(3V)
-shift term, defined in eq. (
81
), we have,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,\Pi_{qq\text{ double-UV}}^{(2,3V)\,\text{shift}}(q,k,l)=ig_{s}^{4}\,C_{A}^{2}(1-\epsilon)\,s^{(3V)}(q,k,l)\\
&\qquad+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,\end{split}

(370)

where the kinematic function
s^{(3V)}
reads,

\displaystyle s^{(3V)}(q,k,l)=-\frac{2k\cdot q}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}(l^{2}-M^{2})}\left(\frac{2\not{l}-3\not{k}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{2(\not{l}-\not{k})}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{4k\cdot l\,\not{l}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\not{q}+\frac{2k\cdot q\,(\not{l}-\not{k})}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\,.

(371)

In addition to the “standard” shift contributions to the quark self-energy, the
q\,||\,p_{2}
Ward identities generate a shift mismatch in terms associated to ghosts,
c.f. eqs. (
225
) and (
227
). These are rendered finite in the double-UV regions using the following counterterms,

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}_{k,l}\,O^{(2)\,\text{shift}}_{2,\text{ double-UV}}(p,q,k,l)=ig_{s}^{5}\,\frac{C_{A}^{2}}{4}\frac{1-\epsilon}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}\Biggl[\frac{4k\cdot q\,\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\not{q}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{2}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{2(k\cdot l)^{2}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})}-1\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\not{k}\not{l}(\not{p}+\not{q})-\not{p}\not{l}\not{k}}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{(l+k)^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{(l-k)\cdot q}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\left(\frac{\not{k}}{l^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{\not{l}}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\quad-\left.\frac{2k\cdot q}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})}\right)\Biggr]+(k\leftrightarrow l)\,,

(372)

and

\displaystyle O^{(2)\,\text{shift}}_{\Delta,2\text{ double-UV}}(q,k,l)=ig_{s}^{5}\,\frac{C_{A}^{2}}{2}\left[\frac{2(1-\epsilon)k\cdot q\,\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{2}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\quad-\left.\frac{(l-k)\cdot q\left(2(1-\epsilon)\not{k}+\not{l}\right)}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}+\frac{k\cdot q\,\not{l}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}\right.

\displaystyle\quad-\left.\frac{(1-\epsilon)\not{q}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\right]

(373)

We note the dependence of eq. (
372
) on the external fermion momentum
p
, which is unusual for a shifted UV counterterm.

Finally, we provide the double-UV subtraction term for self-energy corrections to the fermion propagator with a gluon-triangle subgraph, defined in eq. (
157
),

\displaystyle\Delta\Gamma_{ggg\text{ double-UV}}^{(1)\,\rho,c}(k,l)=g_{s}^{4}\,\left(2\,n_{f}\,s^{\mu}_{n_{f}}(q,k,l)+\frac{C_{A}}{2}\,s^{\mu}_{A}(q,k,l)\right)

(374)

with
s^{\mu}_{n_{f}}
and
s^{\mu}_{A}
are decomposed as,

\displaystyle s^{\mu}_{X}(k,l)=k^{\mu}\,s_{X,k}(k,l)+l^{\mu}\,s_{X,l}(k,l)+\gamma^{\mu}\,s_{X,\gamma}(k,l)\,,\quad X\in\{n_{f},A\}\,,

(375)

where

\displaystyle s_{n_{f},k}(k,l)

\displaystyle=\frac{4\not{k}-2\not{l}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)-\frac{4\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\,k\cdot l(2\not{k}-\not{l})}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}+\frac{16(k\cdot l)^{2}\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{4}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2(1-\epsilon)\not{l}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l-k^{2}-M^{2})}\,,

\displaystyle s_{n_{f},l}(k,l)

\displaystyle=\frac{2(1-\epsilon)\not{l}-2\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)-\frac{4\,k\cdot l\,\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\,,

\displaystyle s_{n_{f},\gamma}(k,l)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{l^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)+\frac{2}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}-\frac{2\,k\cdot l}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\left(1+\frac{2\,k\cdot l}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)\,.

(376)

and

\displaystyle s_{A,k}(k,l)

\displaystyle=2\,\frac{(3-2\epsilon)\not{l}+8(1-\epsilon)(\not{l}-\not{k})}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{2\,k\cdot l}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{16(1-\epsilon)\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\left(1-\frac{4\,(k\cdot l)^{2}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2(11-8\epsilon)\not{l}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}\,,

\displaystyle s_{A,l}(k,l)

\displaystyle=-2\,\frac{\not{k}+9(1-\epsilon)\not{l}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)-\frac{4\,k\cdot l\not{k}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{3}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}

\displaystyle+2\,\frac{\not{k}+4(1-\epsilon)\not{l}}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}\,,

\displaystyle s_{A,\gamma}(k,l)

\displaystyle=-\frac{2}{(l^{2}-M^{2})^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}-M^{2}}-\frac{1}{(l-k)^{2}-M^{2}}\right)-\frac{5}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}\left(\frac{7}{l^{2}-M^{2}}+\frac{2}{k^{2}-M^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\,k\cdot l}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})^{2}}\biggl(\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})(l^{2}-M^{2})}+\frac{1}{(l^{2}-M^{2})((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(k^{2}-M^{2})((l-k)^{2}-M^{2})}+\frac{2\,k\cdot l}{(k^{2}-M^{2})^{2}(l^{2}-M^{2})}\biggr)\,.

(377)

We note that the integrand-level modifications to the gluon three-point function, defined through additive counterterms according to eq. (
156
), do not affect the amplitude in the double-UV region.
